# Reference photo credits (world-car layer)

The reference thumbnails in `web/world_refs/` are downscaled (320 px) copies of photos from [Wikimedia Commons](https://commons.wikimedia.org/), used under their free licences. Each is credited below with its author, licence and source page. They are used only by the world-car layer, never to train or evaluate the 196-class Stanford Cars models.
